You call the remote Faelo MCP tools yourself, in order, gating spend before render. Not for one-shot config runs (use faelo-ship) or environment checks (use faelo-doctor). ([johnnybq/faelo-plugin](https://github.com/johnnybq/faelo-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/faelo/skills/faelo-orchestrate))
- **faelo-ship**: Ship a finished ad in ONE autonomous pass — minimal back-and-forth.
